type PendaftarHandler struct{}

func NewPendaftarHandler() *PendaftarHandler {
        return &PendaftarHandler{}
}

type UpdateIdentitasRequest struct {
        JenisKelamin      string  `json:"jenis_kelamin" binding:"required"`
        Alamat            string  `json:"alamat" binding:"required"`
        TempatLahir       string  `json:"tempat_lahir" binding:"required"`
        TanggalLahir      string  `json:"tanggal_lahir" binding:"required"` // format: YYYY-MM-DD
        StatusWargaNegara string  `json:"status_warga_negara" binding:"required"`
        NegaraWNA         string  `json:"negara_wna"`
        Agama             string  `json:"agama" binding:"required"`
        StatusPernikahan  string  `json:"status_pernikahan" binding:"required"`
}

type UpdateAsalSekolahRequest struct {
        NamaSekolah    string  `json:"nama_sekolah" binding:"required"`
        StatusSekolah  string  `json:"status_sekolah" binding:"required"` // Negeri / Swasta
        JurusanSekolah string  `json:"jurusan_sekolah" binding:"required"`
        NilaiRataRata  float64 `json:"nilai_rata_rata" binding:"required"`
        TahunLulus     int     `json:"tahun_lulus" binding:"required"`
}

type UpdateOrangTuaRequest struct {
        // Ayah
        NamaAyah         string `json:"nama_ayah" binding:"required"`
        NIKAyah          string `json:"nik_ayah"`
        KKAyah           string `json:"kk_ayah"`
        TempatLahirAyah  string `json:"tempat_lahir_ayah"`
        TanggalLahirAyah string `json:"tanggal_lahir_ayah"` // YYYY-MM-DD
        AlamatAyah       string `json:"alamat_ayah"`
        PendidikanAyah   string `json:"pendidikan_ayah"`
        PekerjaanAyah    string `json:"pekerjaan_ayah"`
        PenghasilanAyah  string `json:"penghasilan_ayah"`

        // Ibu
        NamaIbu          string `json:"nama_ibu" binding:"required"`
        NIKIbu           string `json:"nik_ibu"`
        KKIbu            string `json:"kk_ibu"`
        TempatLahirIbu   string `json:"tempat_lahir_ibu"`
        TanggalLahirIbu  string `json:"tanggal_lahir_ibu"` // YYYY-MM-DD
        AlamatIbu        string `json:"alamat_ibu"`
        PendidikanIbu    string `json:"pendidikan_ibu"`
        PekerjaanIbu     string `json:"pekerjaan_ibu"`
        PenghasilanIbu   string `json:"penghasilan_ibu"`

        // Wali (Kondisional)
        NamaWali         string `json:"nama_wali"`
        AlamatWali       string `json:"alamat_wali"`
        NoHPOrangTuaWali string `json:"no_hp_orang_tua_wali"`
}

type UpdateProdiGelombangRequest struct {
        ProgramStudiID   uint   `json:"program_studi_id" binding:"required"`
        GelombangID      uint   `json:"gelombang_id" binding:"required"`
        JalurPendaftaran string `json:"jalur_pendaftaran"`
}

func getPendaftarByUserID(userID uint) (*model.Pendaftar, error) {
        var pendaftar model.Pendaftar
        err := database.DB.Preload("User").
                Preload("Gelombang").
                Preload("ProgramStudi").
                Preload("DataOrangTua").
                Preload("Berkas").
                Preload("Pembayaran").
                Where("user_id = ?", userID).
                First(&pendaftar).Error
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &pendaftar, nil
}

// GetMyPendaftaran returns full registration form data for current applicant (FR-7.1)
func (h *PendaftarHandler) GetMyPendaftaran(c *gin.Context) {
        userID := c.GetUint("user_id")
        pendaftar, err := getPendaftarByUserID(userID)
        if err != nil {
                response.NotFound(c, "Data pendaftaran belum ditemukan")
                return
        }
        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Data pendaftaran berhasil diambil", pendaftar)
}

// UpdateIdentitas updates student identity section (FR-2.1)
func (h *PendaftarHandler) UpdateIdentitas(c *gin.Context) {
        userID := c.GetUint("user_id")
        pendaftar, err := getPendaftarByUserID(userID)
        if err != nil {
                response.NotFound(c, "Data pendaftaran tidak ditemukan")
                return
        }

        if pendaftar.IsLocked {
                response.Forbidden(c, "Data formulir sudah disubmit dan dikunci. Tidak dapat diubah.")
                return
        }

        var req UpdateIdentitasRequest
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Mohon lengkapi seluruh field identitas diri wajib", err.Error())
                return
        }

        var tglLahir *time.Time
        if req.TanggalLahir != "" {
                parsed, err := time.Parse("2006-01-02", req.TanggalLahir)
                if err == nil {
                        tglLahir = &parsed
                }
        }

        pendaftar.JenisKelamin = req.JenisKelamin
        pendaftar.Alamat = req.Alamat
        pendaftar.TempatLahir = req.TempatLahir
        pendaftar.TanggalLahir = tglLahir
        pendaftar.StatusWargaNegara = req.StatusWargaNegara
        pendaftar.NegaraWNA = req.NegaraWNA
        pendaftar.Agama = req.Agama
        pendaftar.StatusPernikahan = req.StatusPernikahan

        if pendaftar.StatusPendaftaran == model.StatusAkunTerdaftar || pendaftar.StatusPendaftaran == model.StatusDraft {
                pendaftar.StatusPendaftaran = model.StatusBelumLengkap
        }

        if err := database.DB.Save(pendaftar).Error; err != nil {
                response.InternalServerError(c, err.Error())
                return
        }

        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Data identitas calon mahasiswa berhasil disimpan", pendaftar)
}

        userID := c.GetUint("user_id")

        }

// UpdateOrangTua updates parent and guardian details (FR-2.3, FR-2.4, FR-2.5)
func (h *PendaftarHandler) UpdateOrangTua(c *gin.Context) {
        userID := c.GetUint("user_id")
        pendaftar, err := getPendaftarByUserID(userID)
        if err != nil {
                response.NotFound(c, "Data pendaftaran tidak ditemukan")
                return
        }

        if pendaftar.IsLocked {
                response.Forbidden(c, "Data formulir sudah disubmit dan dikunci. Tidak dapat diubah.")
                return
        }

        var req UpdateOrangTuaRequest
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Mohon lengkapi data orang tua (Ayah & Ibu) serta kontak", err.Error())
                return
        }

        var tglAyah, tglIbu *time.Time
        if req.TanggalLahirAyah != "" {
                if t, err := time.Parse("2006-01-02", req.TanggalLahirAyah); err == nil {
                        tglAyah = &t
                }
        }
        if req.TanggalLahirIbu != "" {
                if t, err := time.Parse("2006-01-02", req.TanggalLahirIbu); err == nil {
                        tglIbu = &t
                }
        }

        var orangTua model.OrangTua
        err = database.DB.Where("pendaftar_id = ?", pendaftar.ID).First(&orangTua).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                response.InternalServerError(c, err.Error())
                return
        }

        orangTua.PendaftarID = pendaftar.ID
        orangTua.NamaAyah = req.NamaAyah
        orangTua.NIKAyah = req.NIKAyah
        orangTua.KKAyah = req.KKAyah
        orangTua.TempatLahirAyah = req.TempatLahirAyah
        orangTua.TanggalLahirAyah = tglAyah
        orangTua.AlamatAyah = req.AlamatAyah
        orangTua.PendidikanAyah = req.PendidikanAyah
        orangTua.PekerjaanAyah = req.PekerjaanAyah
        orangTua.PenghasilanAyah = req.PenghasilanAyah

        orangTua.NamaIbu = req.NamaIbu
        orangTua.NIKIbu = req.NIKIbu
        orangTua.KKIbu = req.KKIbu
        orangTua.TempatLahirIbu = req.TempatLahirIbu
        orangTua.TanggalLahirIbu = tglIbu
        orangTua.AlamatIbu = req.AlamatIbu
        orangTua.PendidikanIbu = req.PendidikanIbu
        orangTua.PekerjaanIbu = req.PekerjaanIbu
        orangTua.PenghasilanIbu = req.PenghasilanIbu

        orangTua.NamaWali = req.NamaWali
        orangTua.AlamatWali = req.AlamatWali
        orangTua.NoHPOrangTuaWali = req.NoHPOrangTuaWali

        if err := database.DB.Save(&orangTua).Error; err != nil {
                response.InternalServerError(c, err.Error())
                return
        }

        if pendaftar.StatusPendaftaran == model.StatusAkunTerdaftar || pendaftar.StatusPendaftaran == model.StatusDraft {
                pendaftar.StatusPendaftaran = model.StatusBelumLengkap
                database.DB.Save(pendaftar)
        }

        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Data orang tua dan wali berhasil disimpan", orangTua)
}

// UpdatePilihanProdi updates study program and wave (FR-2.6)
func (h *PendaftarHandler) UpdatePilihanProdi(c *gin.Context) {
        userID := c.GetUint("user_id")
        pendaftar, err := getPendaftarByUserID(userID)
        if err != nil {
                response.NotFound(c, "Data pendaftaran tidak ditemukan")
                return
        }

        if pendaftar.IsLocked {
                response.Forbidden(c, "Data formulir sudah disubmit dan dikunci. Tidak dapat diubah.")
                return
        }

        var req UpdateProdiGelombangRequest
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Pilihan program studi dan gelombang harus ditentukan", err.Error())
                return
        }

        pendaftar.ProgramStudiID = &req.ProgramStudiID
        pendaftar.GelombangID = &req.GelombangID
        if req.JalurPendaftaran != "" {
                pendaftar.JalurPendaftaran = req.JalurPendaftaran
        } else if pendaftar.JalurPendaftaran == "" {
                pendaftar.JalurPendaftaran = "Reguler"
        }

        if pendaftar.StatusPendaftaran == model.StatusAkunTerdaftar || pendaftar.StatusPendaftaran == model.StatusDraft {
                pendaftar.StatusPendaftaran = model.StatusBelumLengkap
        }

        if err := database.DB.Save(pendaftar).Error; err != nil {
                response.InternalServerError(c, err.Error())
                return
        }

        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Pilihan program studi & gelombang berhasil diperbarui", pendaftar)
}

// SubmitFinal submits the registration, generates nomor pendaftaran, and locks data (FR-5.5, FR-2.8)
func (h *PendaftarHandler) SubmitFinal(c *gin.Context) {
        userID := c.GetUint("user_id")
        pendaftar, err := getPendaftarByUserID(userID)
        if err != nil {
                response.NotFound(c, "Data pendaftaran tidak ditemukan")
                return
        }

        if pendaftar.IsLocked {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Pendaftaran sudah disubmit sebelumnya.", "Already submitted")
                return
        }

        // Validate completeness
        if pendaftar.JenisKelamin == "" || pendaftar.Alamat == "" || pendaftar.TempatLahir == "" {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Identitas mahasiswa belum lengkap. Silakan lengkapi Bagian 1.", "Incomplete Identity")
                return
        }

        if pendaftar.NamaSekolah == "" || pendaftar.TahunLulus == 0 {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Data asal sekolah belum lengkap. Silakan lengkapi Bagian 2.", "Incomplete School Info")
                return
        }

        if pendaftar.DataOrangTua == nil || pendaftar.DataOrangTua.NamaAyah == "" || pendaftar.DataOrangTua.NamaIbu == "" {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Data orang tua belum lengkap. Silakan lengkapi Bagian 3.", "Incomplete Parent Info")
                return
        }

        if pendaftar.ProgramStudiID == nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Pilihan program studi belum dipilih. Silakan pilih program studi terlebih dahulu.", "Incomplete Prodi")
                return
        }

        // Check mandatory documents (KK, KTP, Ijazah, Foto)
        docTypes := make(map[string]bool)
        for _, b := range pendaftar.Berkas {
                docTypes[strings.ToLower(b.JenisDokumen)] = true
        }

        // Generate Nomor Pendaftaran: PMB-[Tahun]-[Urutan 4 digit] (FR-5.5, Section 8.9)
        currentYear := time.Now().Year()
        prefix := fmt.Sprintf("PMB-%d-", currentYear)

        var countSameYear int64
        database.DB.Model(&model.Pendaftar{}).
                Where("nomor_pendaftaran LIKE ?", prefix+"%").
                Count(&countSameYear)

        nomorPendaftaran := fmt.Sprintf("PMB-%d-%04d", currentYear, countSameYear+1)

        pendaftar.NomorPendaftaran = &nomorPendaftaran
        pendaftar.StatusPendaftaran = model.StatusMenungguPembayaran
        pendaftar.IsLocked = true
        pendaftar.CatatanRevisi = ""

        if err := database.DB.Save(pendaftar).Error; err != nil {
                response.InternalServerError(c, fmt.Sprintf("Gagal menyimpan pendaftaran: %v", err))
                return
        }

        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Pendaftaran berhasil disubmit! Nomor Registrasi PMB Anda telah diterbitkan.", gin.H{
                "nomor_pendaftaran":  nomorPendaftaran,
                "status_pendaftaran": pendaftar.StatusPendaftaran,
                "is_locked":          pendaftar.IsLocked,
        })
}

package handler

import (
        "net/http"
        "strings"

        "pmb-staici-backend/internal/database"
        "pmb-staici-backend/internal/model"
        "pmb-staici-backend/internal/response"

)

type PublicHandler struct{}

func NewPublicHandler() *PublicHandler {
        return &PublicHandler{}
}

// GetInfoPMB returns active waves, programs, and public registration info (FR-10.1)
func (h *PublicHandler) GetInfoPMB(c *gin.Context) {
        var listProdi []model.ProgramStudi
        database.DB.Where("is_aktif = ?", true).Order("id asc").Find(&listProdi)

        var activeGelombang []model.Gelombang
        database.DB.Order("tanggal_buka asc, id asc").Find(&activeGelombang)

        var faqs []model.FAQ
        database.DB.Where("is_aktif = ?", true).Order("urutan asc, id asc").Find(&faqs)

        var settings model.PengaturanSistem
        database.DB.First(&settings)

        // Normalized CMS dynamic tables
        var rekeningList []model.RekeningPembayaran
        database.DB.Where("is_aktif = ?", true).Order("urutan asc, id asc").Find(&rekeningList)

        var jalurList []model.JalurPendaftaran
        database.DB.Where("is_aktif = ?", true).Order("urutan asc, id asc").Find(&jalurList)

        var tataCaraList []model.TataCaraPendaftaran
        database.DB.Where("is_aktif = ?", true).Order("nomor_langkah asc, urutan asc, id asc").Find(&tataCaraList)

        var hotlineList []model.KontakHotline
        database.DB.Where("is_aktif = ?", true).Order("urutan asc, id asc").Find(&hotlineList)

        var persyaratanDokumenList []model.PersyaratanDokumen
        database.DB.Where("is_aktif = ?", true).Order("urutan asc, id asc").Find(&persyaratanDokumenList)

        // Persyaratan list (fallback if empty)
        persyaratanList := []string{
                "Scan/Foto Kartu Keluarga (KK) Asli/Legalisir (Format JPG/PDF maks 2 MB)",
                "Scan/Foto KTP Calon Mahasiswa (Format JPG/PDF maks 2 MB)",
                "Scan/Foto Ijazah / Surat Keterangan Lulus (SKL) SLTA Sederajat",
                "Pas Foto Resmi Berwarna 3x4 (Latar Belakang Merah/Biru)",
                "Bukti Transfer Pembayaran Biaya Pendaftaran PMB yang sah",
                "Scan Kartu KIP / KIP-Kuliah (Khusus Pendaftar Jalur Beasiswa KIP)",
        }
        if len(persyaratanDokumenList) > 0 {
                var docList []string
                for _, doc := range persyaratanDokumenList {
                        label := doc.NamaDokumen
                        if doc.Deskripsi != "" {
                                label += " (" + doc.Deskripsi + ")"
                        }
                        docList = append(docList, label)
                }
                persyaratanList = docList
        } else if settings.PersyaratanPendaftaran != "" {
                lines := strings.Split(settings.PersyaratanPendaftaran, "\n")
                var customList []string
                for _, line := range lines {
                        trimmed := strings.TrimSpace(line)
                        if trimmed != "" {
                                customList = append(customList, trimmed)
                        }
                }
                if len(customList) > 0 {
                        persyaratanList = customList
                }
        }

        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Informasi publik PMB STAICI Situbondo", gin.H{
                "kampus": gin.H{
                        "nama":          "STAI Cendekia Insani Situbondo",
                        "singkatan":     "STAICI",
                        "alamat":        settings.AlamatKampus,
                        "email":         settings.EmailCS,
                        "telepon":       settings.NoWhatsappCS,
                        "jam_layanan":   "Senin - Sabtu: 08.00 - 16.00 WIB",
                        "akreditasi":    "Terakreditasi BAN-PT & Kemenag RI",
                        "website_url":   "https://staicisitubondo.ac.id/pmb",
                },
                "program_studi":          listProdi,
                "gelombang":              activeGelombang,
                "jalur_pendaftaran_info": settings.JalurPendaftaranInfo,
                "jalur_pendaftaran":      jalurList,
                "persyaratan":            persyaratanList,
                "persyaratan_dokumen":    persyaratanDokumenList,
                "tata_cara":              tataCaraList,
                "rekening":               rekeningList,
                "hotline":                hotlineList,
                "faq":                    faqs,
                "settings":               settings,
        })
}

package handler

import (
        "fmt"
        "net/http"
        "strings"

        "pmb-staici-backend/internal/crypto"
        "pmb-staici-backend/internal/database"
        "pmb-staici-backend/internal/model"
        "pmb-staici-backend/internal/response"
        "pmb-staici-backend/internal/util"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

type RBACHandler struct{}

func NewRBACHandler() *RBACHandler {
        return &RBACHandler{}
}

func getRoleFromContext(c *gin.Context) string {
        if roleVal, exists := c.Get("role"); exists {
                switch v := roleVal.(type) {
                case model.Role:
                        return string(v)
                case string:
                        return v
                }
        }
        return ""
}

// GetNavMenus returns dynamic menu groups & allowed menus for the current user's role
func (h *RBACHandler) GetNavMenus(c *gin.Context) {
        roleStr := getRoleFromContext(c)
        if roleStr == "" {
                response.Unauthorized(c, "Role pengguna tidak terautentikasi")
                return
        }

        // Fetch all active menu groups ordered by 'urutan'
        var groups []model.MenuGroup
        if err := database.DB.Where("is_aktif = ?", true).Order("urutan ASC").Find(&groups).Error; err != nil {
                response.InternalServerError(c, err.Error())
                return
        }

        // Fetch allowed Menu IDs for this role
        var allowedRoleMenus []model.RoleMenu
        database.DB.Where("role_kode = ? AND allow_view = ?", roleStr, true).Find(&allowedRoleMenus)

        allowedMenuIDs := make(map[uint]bool)
        for _, rm := range allowedRoleMenus {
                allowedMenuIDs[rm.MenuID] = true
        }

        // Construct Response Groups with allowed Menus
        type MenuResp struct {
                ID          uint   `json:"id"`
                MenuGroupID uint   `json:"menu_group_id"`
                Nama        string `json:"nama"`
                Key         string `json:"key"`
                Icon        string `json:"icon"`
                URL         string `json:"url"`
                BadgeText   string `json:"badge_text"`
                Urutan      int    `json:"urutan"`
        }

        type GroupResp struct {
                ID     uint       `json:"id"`
                Nama   string     `json:"nama"`
                Urutan int        `json:"urutan"`
                Menus  []MenuResp `json:"menus"`
        }

        var result []GroupResp

        for _, g := range groups {
                var menus []model.Menu
                database.DB.Where("menu_group_id = ? AND is_aktif = ?", g.ID, true).Order("urutan ASC").Find(&menus)

                var groupMenus []MenuResp
                for _, m := range menus {
                        // Super admin gets all active menus.
                        // Non-super-admin can NEVER access 'rbac' menu, and only gets explicitly allowed menus
                        if roleStr == string(model.RoleSuperAdmin) {
                                groupMenus = append(groupMenus, MenuResp{
                                        ID:          m.ID,
                                        MenuGroupID: m.MenuGroupID,
                                        Nama:        m.Nama,
                                        Key:         m.Key,
                                        Icon:        m.Icon,
                                        URL:         m.URL,
                                        BadgeText:   m.BadgeText,
                                        Urutan:      m.Urutan,
                                })
                        } else {
                                if m.Key == "rbac" {
                                        continue // Non-super-admin can NEVER receive rbac menu
                                }
                                if allowedMenuIDs[m.ID] {
                                        groupMenus = append(groupMenus, MenuResp{
                                                ID:          m.ID,
                                                MenuGroupID: m.MenuGroupID,
                                                Nama:        m.Nama,
                                                Key:         m.Key,
                                                Icon:        m.Icon,
                                                URL:         m.URL,
                                                BadgeText:   m.BadgeText,
                                                Urutan:      m.Urutan,
                                        })
                                }
                        }
                }

                if len(groupMenus) > 0 {
                        result = append(result, GroupResp{
                                ID:     g.ID,
                                Nama:   g.Nama,
                                Urutan: g.Urutan,
                                Menus:  groupMenus,
                        })
                }
        }

        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Menu navigasi dinamis berhasil dimuat", result)
}

// ListRoles returns all registered roles
func (h *RBACHandler) ListRoles(c *gin.Context) {
        var roles []model.RoleModel
        database.DB.Order("id ASC").Find(&roles)
        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Daftar role pengguna", roles)
}

// CreateRole creates a new custom role
func (h *RBACHandler) CreateRole(c *gin.Context) {
        var req struct {
                Kode      string `json:"kode" binding:"required"`
                Nama      string `json:"nama" binding:"required"`
                Deskripsi string `json:"deskripsi"`
        }

        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Data role tidak valid", err.Error())
                return
        }

        kodeClean := strings.ToLower(strings.ReplaceAll(strings.TrimSpace(req.Kode), " ", "_"))

        var existing model.RoleModel
        if err := database.DB.Where("kode = ?", kodeClean).First(&existing).Error; err == nil {
                response.Error(c, http.StatusConflict, "CONFLICT", "Kode role sudah terdaftar", "")
                return
        }

        newRole := model.RoleModel{
                Kode:      kodeClean,
                Nama:      req.Nama,
                Deskripsi: req.Deskripsi,
                IsSystem:  false,
        }

        if err := database.DB.Create(&newRole).Error; err != nil {
                response.InternalServerError(c, err.Error())
                return
        }

        RecordAdminLog(c, "Tambah Role", "Membuat role baru: "+newRole.Nama)
        response.Success(c, http.StatusCreated, "Role baru berhasil dibuat", newRole)
}

// UpdateRole updates role metadata
func (h *RBACHandler) UpdateRole(c *gin.Context) {
        id := c.Param("id")
        var role model.RoleModel
        if err := database.DB.First(&role, id).Error; err != nil {
                response.NotFound(c, "Role tidak ditemukan")
                return
        }

        var req struct {
                Nama      string `json:"nama"`
                Deskripsi string `json:"deskripsi"`
        }

        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Data tidak valid", err.Error())
                return
        }

        if req.Nama != "" {
                role.Nama = req.Nama
        }
        role.Deskripsi = req.Deskripsi

        database.DB.Save(&role)
        RecordAdminLog(c, "Update Role", "Memperbarui role: "+role.Nama)
        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Role berhasil diperbarui", role)
}

// ListMenuGroups returns all menu groups
func (h *RBACHandler) ListMenuGroups(c *gin.Context) {
        var groups []model.MenuGroup
        database.DB.Preload("Menus").Order("urutan ASC").Find(&groups)
        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Daftar menu group", groups)
}

// CreateMenuGroup creates a new menu group
func (h *RBACHandler) CreateMenuGroup(c *gin.Context) {
        var req struct {
                Nama   string `json:"nama" binding:"required"`
                Urutan int    `json:"urutan"`
        }

        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Data menu group tidak valid", err.Error())
                return
        }

        group := model.MenuGroup{
                Nama:    req.Nama,
                Urutan:  req.Urutan,
                IsAktif: true,
        }

        database.DB.Create(&group)
        RecordAdminLog(c, "Tambah Menu Group", "Membuat menu group baru: "+group.Nama)
        response.Success(c, http.StatusCreated, "Menu group berhasil dibuat", group)
}

// UpdateMenuGroup updates menu group
func (h *RBACHandler) UpdateMenuGroup(c *gin.Context) {
        id := c.Param("id")
        var group model.MenuGroup
        if err := database.DB.First(&group, id).Error; err != nil {
                response.NotFound(c, "Menu group tidak ditemukan")
                return
        }

        var req struct {
                Nama    string `json:"nama"`
                Urutan  int    `json:"urutan"`
                IsAktif *bool  `json:"is_aktif"`
        }

        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Data tidak valid", err.Error())
                return
        }

        if req.Nama != "" {
                group.Nama = req.Nama
        }
        group.Urutan = req.Urutan
        if req.IsAktif != nil {
                group.IsAktif = *req.IsAktif
        }

        database.DB.Save(&group)
        RecordAdminLog(c, "Update Menu Group", "Memperbarui menu group: "+group.Nama)
        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Menu group berhasil diperbarui", group)
}

// DeleteMenuGroup deletes a menu group and its associated menus
func (h *RBACHandler) DeleteMenuGroup(c *gin.Context) {
        id := c.Param("id")
        var group model.MenuGroup
        if err := database.DB.First(&group, id).Error; err != nil {
                response.NotFound(c, "Menu group tidak ditemukan")
                return
        }

        // Safety check: Prevent deleting group containing 'rbac' menu
        var rbacChild model.Menu
        if err := database.DB.Where("menu_group_id = ? AND `key` = ?", group.ID, "rbac").First(&rbacChild).Error; err == nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Menu group ini memuat modul 'rbac' inti sistem dan tidak boleh dihapus", "")
                return
        }

        // Find and remove all child menus and their role associations permanently
        var childMenus []model.Menu
        database.DB.Unscoped().Where("menu_group_id = ?", group.ID).Find(&childMenus)
        for _, m := range childMenus {
                database.DB.Unscoped().Where("menu_id = ?", m.ID).Delete(&model.RoleMenu{})
                database.DB.Unscoped().Delete(&m)
        }

        database.DB.Unscoped().Delete(&group)
        RecordAdminLog(c, "Hapus Menu Group", "Menghapus menu group: "+group.Nama)
        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Menu group beserta menu di dalamnya berhasil dihapus", nil)
}

// ListMenus returns all menus
func (h *RBACHandler) ListMenus(c *gin.Context) {
        var menus []model.Menu
        database.DB.Preload("MenuGroup").Order("menu_group_id ASC, urutan ASC").Find(&menus)
        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Daftar menu", menus)
}

// CreateMenu creates a new menu item
func (h *RBACHandler) CreateMenu(c *gin.Context) {
        var req struct {
                MenuGroupID uint   `json:"menu_group_id" binding:"required"`
                Nama        string `json:"nama" binding:"required"`
                Key         string `json:"key" binding:"required"`
                Icon        string `json:"icon"`
                URL         string `json:"url"`
                BadgeText   string `json:"badge_text"`
                Urutan      int    `json:"urutan"`
        }

        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Data menu tidak valid", err.Error())
                return
        }

        menu := model.Menu{
                MenuGroupID: req.MenuGroupID,
                Nama:        req.Nama,
                Key:         strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(req.Key)),
                Icon:        req.Icon,
                URL:         req.URL,
                BadgeText:   req.BadgeText,
                Urutan:      req.Urutan,
                IsAktif:     true,
        }

        if err := database.DB.Create(&menu).Error; err != nil {
                response.InternalServerError(c, err.Error())
                return
        }

        RecordAdminLog(c, "Tambah Menu", "Membuat menu baru: "+menu.Nama)
        response.Success(c, http.StatusCreated, "Menu berhasil dibuat", menu)
}

// UpdateMenu updates an existing menu item
func (h *RBACHandler) UpdateMenu(c *gin.Context) {
        id := c.Param("id")
        var menu model.Menu
        if err := database.DB.First(&menu, id).Error; err != nil {
                response.NotFound(c, "Menu tidak ditemukan")
                return
        }

        var req struct {
                MenuGroupID uint   `json:"menu_group_id"`
                Nama        string `json:"nama"`
                Key         string `json:"key"`
                Icon        string `json:"icon"`
                URL         string `json:"url"`
                BadgeText   string `json:"badge_text"`
                Urutan      int    `json:"urutan"`
                IsAktif     *bool  `json:"is_aktif"`
        }

        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Data menu tidak valid", err.Error())
                return
        }

        if req.MenuGroupID > 0 {
                menu.MenuGroupID = req.MenuGroupID
        }
        if req.Nama != "" {
                menu.Nama = req.Nama
        }
        // Prevent changing key if it's the core system 'rbac' menu
        if req.Key != "" && menu.Key != "rbac" {
                cleanKey := strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(req.Key))
                menu.Key = cleanKey
                menu.URL = cleanKey
        }
        if req.Icon != "" {
                menu.Icon = req.Icon
        }
        if req.URL != "" && menu.Key != "rbac" {
                menu.URL = req.URL
        }
        menu.BadgeText = req.BadgeText
        if req.Urutan > 0 {
                menu.Urutan = req.Urutan
        }
        if req.IsAktif != nil {
                menu.IsAktif = *req.IsAktif
        }

        database.DB.Save(&menu)
        RecordAdminLog(c, "Update Menu", "Memperbarui menu: "+menu.Nama)
        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Menu berhasil diperbarui", menu)
}

// DeleteMenu deletes a menu item
func (h *RBACHandler) DeleteMenu(c *gin.Context) {
        id := c.Param("id")
        var menu model.Menu
        if err := database.DB.First(&menu, id).Error; err != nil {
                response.NotFound(c, "Menu tidak ditemukan")
                return
        }

        // Safety check: 'rbac' menu cannot be deleted
        if menu.Key == "rbac" || menu.URL == "rbac" {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Menu 'rbac' adalah menu sistem inti dan tidak boleh dihapus", "")
                return
        }

        // Hard delete role permissions for this menu and the menu itself
        database.DB.Unscoped().Where("menu_id = ?", menu.ID).Delete(&model.RoleMenu{})
        database.DB.Unscoped().Delete(&menu)

        RecordAdminLog(c, "Hapus Menu", "Menghapus menu permanen: "+menu.Nama)
        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Menu berhasil dihapus permanen", nil)
}

// GetRolePermissions returns allowed menu IDs for a role
func (h *RBACHandler) GetRolePermissions(c *gin.Context) {
        roleKode := c.Param("role_kode")
        var roleMenus []model.RoleMenu
        database.DB.Where("role_kode = ?", roleKode).Find(&roleMenus)

        allowedMenuIDs := []uint{}
        for _, rm := range roleMenus {
                if rm.AllowView {
                        allowedMenuIDs = append(allowedMenuIDs, rm.MenuID)
                }
        }

        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Hak akses menu role", gin.H{
                "role_kode":        roleKode,
                "allowed_menu_ids": allowedMenuIDs,
        })
}

// UpdateRolePermissions assigns allowed menu IDs to a role
func (h *RBACHandler) UpdateRolePermissions(c *gin.Context) {
        var req struct {
                RoleKode string `json:"role_kode" binding:"required"`
                MenuIDs  []uint `json:"menu_ids"`
        }

        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Data tidak valid", err.Error())
                return
        }

        // Safety check: 'rbac' menu ID should NEVER be assigned to non-super_admin roles!
        var rbacMenu model.Menu
        if err := database.DB.Where("`key` = ?", "rbac").First(&rbacMenu).Error; err == nil {
                if req.RoleKode != string(model.RoleSuperAdmin) {
                        var filteredIDs []uint
                        for _, id := range req.MenuIDs {
                                if id != rbacMenu.ID {
                                        filteredIDs = append(filteredIDs, id)
                                }
                        }
                        req.MenuIDs = filteredIDs
                }
        }

        // Remove old permissions
        database.DB.Where("role_kode = ?", req.RoleKode).Delete(&model.RoleMenu{})

        // Insert new permissions
        for _, menuID := range req.MenuIDs {
                database.DB.Create(&model.RoleMenu{
                        RoleKode:  req.RoleKode,
                        MenuID:    menuID,
                        AllowView: true,
                })
        }

        RecordAdminLog(c, "Update Permisi RBAC", "Memperbarui hak akses menu untuk role: "+req.RoleKode)
        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Hak akses menu role berhasil diperbarui", nil)
}

// ListAdminUsers returns all non-student admin users
func (h *RBACHandler) ListAdminUsers(c *gin.Context) {
        var users []model.User
        database.DB.Where("role != ?", model.RoleCalonMahasiswa).Order("id ASC").Find(&users)
        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Daftar pengguna administrator", users)
}

// UpdateUserRole updates an admin's role
func (h *RBACHandler) UpdateUserRole(c *gin.Context) {
        id := c.Param("id")
        var user model.User
        if err := database.DB.First(&user, id).Error; err != nil {
                response.NotFound(c, "Pengguna tidak ditemukan")
                return
        }

        var req struct {
                Role model.Role `json:"role" binding:"required"`
        }

        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Role tidak valid", err.Error())
                return
        }

        // Prevent demoting the last super admin
        if user.Role == model.RoleSuperAdmin && req.Role != model.RoleSuperAdmin {
                var saCount int64
                database.DB.Model(&model.User{}).Where("role = ?", model.RoleSuperAdmin).Count(&saCount)
                if saCount <= 1 {
                        response.BadRequest(c, "Tidak dapat mengubah role Super Admin terakhir dalam sistem", "Sistem membutuhkan minimal 1 akun Super Admin")
                        return
                }
        }

        oldRole := user.Role
        user.Role = req.Role
        database.DB.Save(&user)

        RecordAdminLog(c, "Ubah Role Admin", fmt.Sprintf("Admin %s (ID %d) diubah dari %s ke %s", user.NamaLengkap, user.ID, oldRole, user.Role))
        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Role pengguna berhasil diperbarui", user)
}

// CreateAdminUser creates a new admin user
func (h *RBACHandler) CreateAdminUser(c *gin.Context) {
        var req struct {
                NamaLengkap string     `json:"nama_lengkap" binding:"required"`
                NIK         string     `json:"nik" binding:"required"`
                Email       string     `json:"email" binding:"required"`
                Password    string     `json:"password" binding:"required"`
                NoHP        string     `json:"no_hp"`
                Role        model.Role `json:"role" binding:"required"`
        }

        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Data pengguna tidak valid", err.Error())
                return
        }

        nikIdx := crypto.BlindIndex(req.NIK)
        var countNIK int64
        database.DB.Model(&model.User{}).Where("nik_idx = ? OR nik = ?", nikIdx, req.NIK).Count(&countNIK)
        if countNIK > 0 {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Username / NIK sudah terdaftar", "Gunakan NIK atau username lain")
                return
        }

        var countEmail int64
        database.DB.Model(&model.User{}).Where("email = ?", req.Email).Count(&countEmail)
        if countEmail > 0 {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Email sudah terdaftar", "Gunakan email lain")
                return
        }

        hash, err := util.HashPassword(req.Password)
        if err != nil {
                response.InternalServerError(c, "Gagal mengenkripsi kata sandi")
                return
        }

        newUser := model.User{
                NamaLengkap:     req.NamaLengkap,
                NIK:             req.NIK,
                Email:           req.Email,
                NoHP:            req.NoHP,
                PasswordHash:    hash,
                Role:            req.Role,
                IsEmailVerified: true,
        }

        if err := database.DB.Create(&newUser).Error; err != nil {
                response.InternalServerError(c, err.Error())
                return
        }

        RecordAdminLog(c, "Tambah Akun Admin", fmt.Sprintf("Membuat akun admin baru: %s (%s)", newUser.NamaLengkap, newUser.Role))
        response.Success(c, http.StatusCreated, "Akun administrator baru berhasil dibuat", newUser)
}

package handler

import (
        "fmt"
        "net/http"
        "strconv"

        "pmb-staici-backend/internal/database"
        "pmb-staici-backend/internal/model"
        "pmb-staici-backend/internal/response"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

type TranslationHandler struct{}

func NewTranslationHandler() *TranslationHandler {
        return &TranslationHandler{}
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────
// PUBLIC: GET /api/v1/public/translations
// Mengembalikan seluruh teks terjemahan terformat
// sebagai nested JSON {group: {key: {id, en}}}
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────

func (h *TranslationHandler) GetPublicTranslations(c *gin.Context) {
        var rows []model.SiteTranslation
        database.DB.Order("group_name asc, urutan asc, id asc").Find(&rows)

        // Bangun struktur nested: { "navbar": { "beranda": { "id": "...", "en": "..." } } }
        result := make(map[string]map[string]map[string]string)
        for _, r := range rows {
                if result[r.GroupName] == nil {
                        result[r.GroupName] = make(map[string]map[string]string)
                }
                result[r.GroupName][r.TranslationKey] = map[string]string{
                        "id": r.TextID,
                        "en": r.TextEN,
                }
        }

        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Teks terjemahan publik", result)
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────
// ADMIN: LIST semua terjemahan (flat array)
// GET /api/v1/admin/translations
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────

func (h *TranslationHandler) ListTranslations(c *gin.Context) {
        groupFilter := c.Query("group")

        var list []model.SiteTranslation
        q := database.DB.Order("group_name asc, urutan asc, id asc")
        if groupFilter != "" {
                q = q.Where("group_name = ?", groupFilter)
        }
        q.Find(&list)

        // Kumpulkan daftar grup unik untuk filter di frontend
        var groups []string
        database.DB.Model(&model.SiteTranslation{}).
                Distinct("group_name").
                Order("group_name asc").
                Pluck("group_name", &groups)

        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Daftar teks terjemahan", gin.H{
                "translations": list,
                "groups":       groups,
        })
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────
// ADMIN: CREATE terjemahan baru
// POST /api/v1/admin/translations
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────

func (h *TranslationHandler) CreateTranslation(c *gin.Context) {
        var req model.SiteTranslation
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Data terjemahan tidak valid", err.Error())
                return
        }

        if req.GroupName == "" || req.TranslationKey == "" {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Nama grup dan kunci terjemahan wajib diisi", "validation_failed")
                return
        }
        if req.TextID == "" {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Teks Bahasa Indonesia wajib diisi", "validation_failed")
                return
        }

        // Cek duplikat
        var existing model.SiteTranslation
        if err := database.DB.Where("group_name = ? AND translation_key = ?", req.GroupName, req.TranslationKey).First(&existing).Error; err == nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, fmt.Sprintf("Kunci '%s.%s' sudah ada di database", req.GroupName, req.TranslationKey), "duplicate_key")
                return
        }

        if err := database.DB.Create(&req).Error; err != nil {
                response.InternalServerError(c, err.Error())
                return
        }

        RecordAdminLog(c, "Tambah Terjemahan", fmt.Sprintf("Admin menambahkan teks: %s.%s", req.GroupName, req.TranslationKey))
        response.Success(c, http.StatusCreated, "Teks terjemahan berhasil ditambahkan", req)
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────
// ADMIN: UPDATE terjemahan
// PUT /api/v1/admin/translations/:id
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────

func (h *TranslationHandler) UpdateTranslation(c *gin.Context) {
        idStr := c.Param("id")
        id, err := strconv.ParseUint(idStr, 10, 32)
        if err != nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "ID terjemahan tidak valid", err.Error())
                return
        }

        var existing model.SiteTranslation
        if err := database.DB.First(&existing, id).Error; err != nil {
                response.NotFound(c, "Teks terjemahan tidak ditemukan")
                return
        }

        var req model.SiteTranslation
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Data terjemahan tidak valid", err.Error())
                return
        }

        existing.GroupName = req.GroupName
        existing.TranslationKey = req.TranslationKey
        existing.TextID = req.TextID
        existing.TextEN = req.TextEN
        if req.Urutan != 0 {
                existing.Urutan = req.Urutan
        }

        if err := database.DB.Save(&existing).Error; err != nil {
                response.InternalServerError(c, err.Error())
                return
        }

        RecordAdminLog(c, "Update Terjemahan", fmt.Sprintf("Admin memperbarui teks: %s.%s", existing.GroupName, existing.TranslationKey))
        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Teks terjemahan berhasil diperbarui", existing)
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────
// ADMIN: DELETE terjemahan
// DELETE /api/v1/admin/translations/:id
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────

func (h *TranslationHandler) DeleteTranslation(c *gin.Context) {
        idStr := c.Param("id")
        id, err := strconv.ParseUint(idStr, 10, 32)
        if err != nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "ID terjemahan tidak valid", err.Error())
                return
        }

        var existing model.SiteTranslation
        if err := database.DB.First(&existing, id).Error; err != nil {
                response.NotFound(c, "Teks terjemahan tidak ditemukan")
                return
        }

        database.DB.Delete(&existing)

        RecordAdminLog(c, "Hapus Terjemahan", fmt.Sprintf("Admin menghapus teks: %s.%s", existing.GroupName, existing.TranslationKey))
        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, "Teks terjemahan berhasil dihapus", nil)
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────
// ADMIN: BULK UPDATE (untuk simpan massal dari tab CMS)
// PUT /api/v1/admin/translations/bulk
// Body: { "translations": [ { id, text_id, text_en }, ... ] }
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────

func (h *TranslationHandler) BulkUpdateTranslations(c *gin.Context) {
        var req struct {
                Translations []struct {
                        ID     uint   `json:"id"`
                        TextID string `json:"text_id"`
                        TextEN string `json:"text_en"`
                } `json:"translations"`
        }

        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&req); err != nil {
                response.BadRequest(c, "Data bulk update tidak valid", err.Error())
                return
        }

        updatedCount := 0
        for _, item := range req.Translations {
                if item.ID == 0 {
                        continue
                }
                result := database.DB.Model(&model.SiteTranslation{}).
                        Where("id = ?", item.ID).
                        Updates(map[string]interface{}{
                                "text_id": item.TextID,
                                "text_en": item.TextEN,
                        })
                if result.RowsAffected > 0 {
                        updatedCount++
                }
        }

        RecordAdminLog(c, "Bulk Update Terjemahan", fmt.Sprintf("Admin memperbarui %d baris teks terjemahan secara massal", updatedCount))
        response.Success(c, http.StatusOK, fmt.Sprintf("Berhasil memperbarui %d teks terjemahan", updatedCount), gin.H{
                "updated_count": updatedCount,
        })
}

package middleware

import (
        "fmt"
        "net/http"
        "strings"

        "pmb-staici-backend/internal/config"
        "pmb-staici-backend/internal/model"
        "pmb-staici-backend/internal/response"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/golang-jwt/jwt/v5"
)

type Claims struct {
        UserID uint       `json:"user_id"`
        Email  string     `json:"email"`
        Role   model.Role `json:"role"`
        jwt.RegisteredClaims
}

func AuthMiddleware(cfg *config.Config) gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                authHeader := c.GetHeader("Authorization")
                if authHeader == "" {
                        c.JSON(http.StatusUnauthorized, gin.H{"error": "Authorization header diperlukan"})
                        c.Abort()
                        return
                }

                parts := strings.Split(authHeader, " ")
                if len(parts) != 2 || parts[0] != "Bearer" {
                        c.JSON(http.StatusUnauthorized, gin.H{"error": "Format token tidak valid (Gunakan Bearer <token>)"})
                        c.Abort()
                        return
                }

                tokenString := parts[1]
                claims := &Claims{}

                token, err := jwt.ParseWithClaims(tokenString, claims, func(token *jwt.Token) (interface{}, error) {
                        if _, ok := token.Method.(*jwt.SigningMethodHMAC); !ok {
                                return nil, fmt.Errorf("metode signing tidak terduga: %v", token.Header["alg"])
                        }
                        return []byte(cfg.JWTSecret), nil
                })

                if err != nil || !token.Valid {
                        c.JSON(http.StatusUnauthorized, gin.H{"error": "Token tidak valid atau telah kedaluwarsa"})
                        c.Abort()
                        return
                }

                c.Set("user_id", claims.UserID)
                c.Set("email", claims.Email)
                c.Set("role", string(claims.Role))
                c.Set("role_model", claims.Role)
                c.Next()
        }
}

// RequireRoles membatasi akses endpoint berdasarkan peran RBAC
func RequireRoles(roles ...model.Role) gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                roleVal, exists := c.Get("role")
                if !exists {
                        response.Forbidden(c, "Akses ditolak: sesi login Anda tidak memiliki peran (role) yang valid")
                        c.Abort()
                        return
                }

                var userRole model.Role
                switch v := roleVal.(type) {
                case model.Role:
                        userRole = v
                case string:
                        userRole = model.Role(v)
                default:
                        response.Forbidden(c, "Akses ditolak: format role tidak valid")
                        c.Abort()
                        return
                }

                for _, r := range roles {
                        if userRole == r {
                                c.Next()
                                return
                        }
                }

                response.Forbidden(c, fmt.Sprintf("Akses ditolak: Anda login sebagai '%s', tindakan ini khusus untuk Super Administrator", userRole))
                c.Abort()
        }
}

// RequireAdminStaff memastikan pengguna memiliki peran pengelola/admin (bukan calon mahasiswa)
func RequireAdminStaff() gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                roleVal, exists := c.Get("role")
                if !exists {
                        response.Forbidden(c, "Akses ditolak: sesi login tidak valid")
                        c.Abort()
                        return
                }

                roleStr := fmt.Sprintf("%v", roleVal)
                if roleStr == string(model.RoleCalonMahasiswa) || roleStr == "" {
                        response.Forbidden(c, "Akses ditolak: Portal ini khusus untuk Administrator dan Pengelola PMB")
                        c.Abort()
                        return
                }

                c.Next()
        }
}

package middleware

import (
        "net/http"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

func CORSMiddleware() gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                c.Writer.Header().Set("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*")
                c.Writer.Header().Set("Access-Control-Allow-Credentials", "true")
                c.Writer.Header().Set("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Content-Type, Content-Length, Accept-Encoding, X-CSRF-Token, Authorization, accept, origin, Cache-Control, X-Requested-With")
                c.Writer.Header().Set("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "POST, OPTIONS, GET, PUT, PATCH, DELETE")

                if c.Request.Method == "OPTIONS" {
                        c.AbortWithStatus(http.StatusNoContent)
                        return
                }

                c.Next()
        }
}

package middleware

import (
        "bytes"
        "io"
        "log"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/google/uuid"
)

// StructuredLogger logs HTTP requests with Request ID, sanitizing sensitive fields (PRD 7.7)
func StructuredLogger() gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                reqID := c.GetHeader("X-Request-ID")
                if reqID == "" {
                        reqID = uuid.New().String()
                }
                c.Header("X-Request-ID", reqID)
                c.Set("request_id", reqID)

                startTime := time.Now()
                path := c.Request.URL.Path
                rawQuery := c.Request.URL.RawQuery

                // Process request
                c.Next()

                latency := time.Since(startTime)
                statusCode := c.Writer.Status()
                clientIP := c.ClientIP()
                method := c.Request.Method

                // Log entry without raw passwords or tokens
                if rawQuery != "" {
                        path = path + "?" + rawQuery
                }

                userEmail, _ := c.Get("email")
                userID, _ := c.Get("user_id")

                log.Printf("[REQ:%s] %s %s | %d | %v | IP:%s | UID:%v (%v)",
                        reqID, method, path, statusCode, latency, clientIP, userID, userEmail)
        }
}

// Helper to safely read and restore request body if needed
func SafeReadBody(c *gin.Context) []byte {
        var bodyBytes []byte
        if c.Request.Body != nil {
                bodyBytes, _ = io.ReadAll(c.Request.Body)
        }
        c.Request.Body = io.NopCloser(bytes.NewBuffer(bodyBytes))
        return bodyBytes
}

// SanitizeLogText removes NIK, passwords, tokens from string logs
func SanitizeLogText(text string) string {
        sensitiveKeys := []string{"password", "kata_sandi", "token", "nik"}
        result := text
        for _, k := range sensitiveKeys {
                if strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(result), k) {
                        result = strings.ReplaceAll(result, k, "[REDACTED]")
                }
        }
        return result
}

        RoleAdminPMB       Role = "admin_pmb"
        RoleSuperAdmin     Role = "super_admin"
)

        StatusDraft               StatusPendaftaran = "draft"
        StatusAkunTerdaftar       StatusPendaftaran = "akun_terdaftar"
        StatusBelumLengkap        StatusPendaftaran = "belum_lengkap"
        StatusMenungguPembayaran  StatusPendaftaran = "menunggu_pembayaran"
        StatusMenungguVerifikasi  StatusPendaftaran = "menunggu_verifikasi"
        StatusBerkasDiverifikasi  StatusPendaftaran = "berkas_diverifikasi"
        StatusDitolak             StatusPendaftaran = "ditolak_perlu_perbaikan"
        StatusMengikutiUjian      StatusPendaftaran = "mengikuti_ujian"
        StatusLolosSeleksi        StatusPendaftaran = "lolos_seleksi"
        StatusTidakLolos          StatusPendaftaran = "tidak_lolos"
        StatusDaftarUlang         StatusPendaftaran = "daftar_ulang"
        StatusKadaluarsa          StatusPendaftaran = "kadaluarsa"
)

        StatusBerkasPending  StatusVerifikasiBerkas = "pending"
        StatusBerkasValid    StatusVerifikasiBerkas = "valid"
        StatusBerkasInvalid  StatusVerifikasiBerkas = "invalid"
)

        StatusBayarMenungguVerifikasi StatusPembayaran = "menunggu_verifikasi"

type User struct {
        ID                 uint           `gorm:"primaryKey" json:"id"`
        Email              string         `gorm:"type:varchar(191);uniqueIndex;not null" json:"email"`
        PasswordHash       string         `gorm:"type:varchar(255);not null" json:"-"`
        NamaLengkap        string         `gorm:"type:varchar(191);not null" json:"nama_lengkap"`
        NoHP               string         `gorm:"type:varchar(30);not null" json:"no_hp"`
        UUID               string         `gorm:"type:varchar(36);index" json:"uuid"`
        NIK                string         `gorm:"type:varchar(255);not null" json:"nik"`
        NIKIdx             string         `gorm:"type:varchar(64);index" json:"-"`
        Role               Role           `gorm:"type:varchar(30);default:'calon_mahasiswa'" json:"role"`
        FotoProfil         string         `gorm:"type:varchar(255)" json:"foto_profil,omitempty"`
        IsEmailVerified    bool           `gorm:"default:false" json:"is_email_verified"`
        VerificationToken  string         `gorm:"type:varchar(255)" json:"-"`
        VerificationOTP    string         `gorm:"type:varchar(10)" json:"-"`
        OTPExpiresAt       *time.Time     `json:"-"`
        ResetPasswordToken     string         `gorm:"type:varchar(255)" json:"-"`
        ResetPasswordExpiresAt *time.Time     `json:"-"`
        CreatedAt              time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
        UpdatedAt              time.Time      `json:"updated_at"`
        DeletedAt              gorm.DeletedAt `gorm:"index" json:"-"`
}

        ID                  uint           `gorm:"primaryKey" json:"id"`
        Kode                string         `gorm:"type:varchar(20);uniqueIndex;not null" json:"kode"` // MPI, AS, KPI
        Nama                string         `gorm:"type:varchar(191);not null" json:"nama"`
        NamaEN              string         `gorm:"type:varchar(191)" json:"nama_en"`
        Jenjang             string         `gorm:"type:varchar(10);default:'S1'" json:"jenjang"`
        GelarAkademik       string         `gorm:"type:varchar(255)" json:"gelar_akademik"`
        GelarAkademikEN     string         `gorm:"type:varchar(255)" json:"gelar_akademik_en"`
        Deskripsi           string         `gorm:"type:text" json:"deskripsi"`
        DeskripsiEN         string         `gorm:"type:text" json:"deskripsi_en"`
        ProfilLulusanIntro  string         `gorm:"type:text" json:"profil_lulusan_intro"`
        ProfilLulusanIntroEN string        `gorm:"type:text" json:"profil_lulusan_intro_en"`
        ProfilLulusan       string         `gorm:"type:longtext" json:"profil_lulusan"`        // JSON string of [{title, desc}]
        CapaianPembelajaran string         `gorm:"type:longtext" json:"capaian_pembelajaran"` // JSON string of [{title, desc}]
        AlurPendaftaran     string         `gorm:"type:text" json:"alur_pendaftaran"`
        IsAktif             bool           `gorm:"default:true" json:"is_aktif"`
        CreatedAt           time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
        UpdatedAt           time.Time      `json:"updated_at"`
        DeletedAt           gorm.DeletedAt `gorm:"index" json:"-"`
}

type Gelombang struct {
        ID               uint           `gorm:"primaryKey" json:"id"`
        Nama             string         `gorm:"type:varchar(100);not null" json:"nama"` // contoh: "Gelombang 1 - 2026/2027"
        NamaEN           string         `gorm:"type:varchar(100)" json:"nama_en"`

        CreatedAt        time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
        UpdatedAt        time.Time      `json:"updated_at"`
        DeletedAt        gorm.DeletedAt `gorm:"index" json:"-"`
}

type Pendaftar struct {
        ID                 uint              `gorm:"primaryKey" json:"id"`
        UserID             uint              `gorm:"uniqueIndex;not null" json:"user_id"`
        User               User              `gorm:"foreignKey:UserID" json:"user,omitempty"`
        GelombangID        *uint             `gorm:"index" json:"gelombang_id"`
        Gelombang          *Gelombang        `gorm:"foreignKey:GelombangID" json:"gelombang,omitempty"`
        ProgramStudiID     *uint             `gorm:"index" json:"program_studi_id"`
        ProgramStudi       *ProgramStudi     `gorm:"foreignKey:ProgramStudiID" json:"program_studi,omitempty"`
        NomorPendaftaran   *string           `gorm:"type:varchar(50);uniqueIndex" json:"nomor_pendaftaran"` // format: PMB-2026-0001
        JalurPendaftaran   string            `gorm:"type:varchar(50)" json:"jalur_pendaftaran"` // Reguler / KIP-Kuliah
        StatusPendaftaran  StatusPendaftaran `gorm:"type:varchar(40);default:'akun_terdaftar'" json:"status_pendaftaran"`
        CatatanRevisi      string            `gorm:"type:text" json:"catatan_revisi,omitempty"`
        IsLocked           bool              `gorm:"default:false" json:"is_locked"` // kunci data setelah submit

        // Identitas Mahasiswa (FR-2.1)
        JenisKelamin       string            `gorm:"type:varchar(20)" json:"jenis_kelamin"`
        Alamat             string            `gorm:"type:text" json:"alamat"`
        TempatLahir        string            `gorm:"type:varchar(100)" json:"tempat_lahir"`
        TanggalLahir       *time.Time        `json:"tanggal_lahir"`
        StatusWargaNegara  string            `gorm:"type:varchar(10);default:'WNI'" json:"status_warga_negara"`
        NegaraWNA          string            `gorm:"type:varchar(100)" json:"negara_wna,omitempty"`
        Agama              string            `gorm:"type:varchar(30);default:'Islam'" json:"agama"`
        StatusPernikahan   string            `gorm:"type:varchar(30)" json:"status_pernikahan"`

        // Asal Sekolah SLTA (FR-2.2)
        NamaSekolah        string            `gorm:"type:varchar(191)" json:"nama_sekolah"`
        StatusSekolah      string            `gorm:"type:varchar(20)" json:"status_sekolah"` // Negeri / Swasta
        JurusanSekolah     string            `gorm:"type:varchar(100)" json:"jurusan_sekolah"`
        NilaiRataRata      float64           `gorm:"type:decimal(5,2)" json:"nilai_rata_rata"`
        TahunLulus         int               `json:"tahun_lulus"`

        // Penilaian Seleksi & Kelulusan (FR-6.2, FR-8.5)
        NilaiUjian         *float64          `gorm:"type:decimal(5,2)" json:"nilai_ujian,omitempty"`
        NilaiWawancara     *float64          `gorm:"type:decimal(5,2)" json:"nilai_wawancara,omitempty"`
        CatatanSeleksi     string            `gorm:"type:text" json:"catatan_seleksi,omitempty"`

        // Relasi
        DataOrangTua       *OrangTua         `gorm:"foreignKey:PendaftarID" json:"data_orang_tua,omitempty"`
        Berkas             []Berkas          `gorm:"foreignKey:PendaftarID" json:"berkas,omitempty"`
        Pembayaran         []Pembayaran      `gorm:"foreignKey:PendaftarID" json:"pembayaran,omitempty"`

        CreatedAt          time.Time         `json:"created_at"`
        UpdatedAt          time.Time         `json:"updated_at"`
        DeletedAt          gorm.DeletedAt    `gorm:"index" json:"-"`
}

func (p *Pendaftar) DisplayNomorPendaftaran() string {
        if p != nil && p.NomorPendaftaran != nil && *p.NomorPendaftaran != "" {
                return *p.NomorPendaftaran
        }
        return "-"
}

        PekerjaanAyah        string    `gorm:"type:varchar(100)" json:"pekerjaan_ayah"`
        PenghasilanAyah      string    `gorm:"type:varchar(100)" json:"penghasilan_ayah"`

        // Data Ibu (FR-2.3)

        NIKIbu               string    `gorm:"type:varchar(255)" json:"nik_ibu"`
        NIKIbuIdx            string    `gorm:"type:varchar(64);index" json:"-"`
        KKIbu                string    `gorm:"type:varchar(255)" json:"kk_ibu"`
        KKIbuIdx             string    `gorm:"type:varchar(64);index" json:"-"`

}

// PersyaratanDokumen model untuk master dokumen persyaratan pendaftaran
type PersyaratanDokumen struct {
        ID          uint      `gorm:"primaryKey" json:"id"`
        Kode        string    `gorm:"type:varchar(50);not null" json:"kode"`
        NamaDokumen   string    `gorm:"type:varchar(191);not null" json:"nama_dokumen"`
        NamaDokumenEN string    `gorm:"type:varchar(191)" json:"nama_dokumen_en"`
        Deskripsi     string    `gorm:"type:text" json:"deskripsi"`
        DeskripsiEN   string    `gorm:"type:text" json:"deskripsi_en"`
        IsWajib     bool      `gorm:"default:true" json:"is_wajib"`
        JalurKhusus string    `gorm:"type:varchar(50);default:'semua'" json:"jalur_khusus"` // semua / kip-kuliah / reguler
        TipeFile    string    `gorm:"type:varchar(100);default:'jpg,jpeg,png,pdf'" json:"tipe_file"`
        MaxSizeMB   int       `gorm:"default:2" json:"max_size_mb"`
        IsAktif     bool      `gorm:"default:true" json:"is_aktif"`
        Urutan      int       `gorm:"default:0" json:"urutan"`
        CreatedAt   time.Time `json:"created_at"`
        UpdatedAt   time.Time `json:"updated_at"`
}


// RBAC System Structs
type RoleModel struct {
        ID        uint           `gorm:"primaryKey" json:"id"`
        Kode      string         `gorm:"type:varchar(50);uniqueIndex;not null" json:"kode"`
        Nama      string         `gorm:"type:varchar(100);not null" json:"nama"`
        Deskripsi string         `gorm:"type:text" json:"deskripsi"`
        IsSystem  bool           `gorm:"default:false" json:"is_system"`
        CreatedAt time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
        UpdatedAt time.Time      `json:"updated_at"`
        DeletedAt gorm.DeletedAt `gorm:"index" json:"-"`
}

type MenuGroup struct {
        ID        uint           `gorm:"primaryKey" json:"id"`
        Nama      string         `gorm:"type:varchar(100);not null" json:"nama"`
        Urutan    int            `gorm:"default:0" json:"urutan"`
        IsAktif   bool           `gorm:"default:true" json:"is_aktif"`
        CreatedAt time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
        UpdatedAt time.Time      `json:"updated_at"`
        DeletedAt gorm.DeletedAt `gorm:"index" json:"-"`
        Menus     []Menu         `gorm:"foreignKey:MenuGroupID" json:"menus,omitempty"`
}

type Menu struct {
        ID          uint           `gorm:"primaryKey" json:"id"`
        MenuGroupID uint           `gorm:"index;not null" json:"menu_group_id"`
        MenuGroup   *MenuGroup     `gorm:"foreignKey:MenuGroupID" json:"menu_group,omitempty"`
        Nama        string         `gorm:"type:varchar(100);not null" json:"nama"`
        Key         string         `gorm:"type:varchar(50);uniqueIndex;not null" json:"key"`
        Icon        string         `gorm:"type:varchar(50);default:'Users'" json:"icon"`
        URL         string         `gorm:"type:varchar(191)" json:"url"`
        BadgeText   string         `gorm:"type:varchar(50)" json:"badge_text"`
        Urutan      int            `gorm:"default:0" json:"urutan"`
        IsAktif     bool           `gorm:"default:true" json:"is_aktif"`
        CreatedAt   time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
        UpdatedAt   time.Time      `json:"updated_at"`
        DeletedAt   gorm.DeletedAt `gorm:"index" json:"-"`
}

type RoleMenu struct {
        ID        uint      `gorm:"primaryKey" json:"id"`
        RoleKode  string    `gorm:"type:varchar(50);index;not null" json:"role_kode"`
        MenuID    uint      `gorm:"index;not null" json:"menu_id"`
        Menu      *Menu     `gorm:"foreignKey:MenuID" json:"menu,omitempty"`
        AllowView bool      `gorm:"default:true" json:"allow_view"`
        CreatedAt time.Time `json:"created_at"`
        UpdatedAt time.Time `json:"updated_at"`
}

// SiteTranslation menyimpan teks terjemahan yang dikelola oleh admin CMS secara dinamis.
// Setiap baris mewakili satu kunci teks dengan nilai Bahasa Indonesia (TextID) dan Bahasa Inggris (TextEN).
type SiteTranslation struct {
        ID             uint      `gorm:"primaryKey" json:"id"`
        GroupName      string    `gorm:"type:varchar(50);not null;uniqueIndex:idx_group_key" json:"group_name"`      // e.g. navbar, hero, footer, cta, alur, sidebar, pendaftaranPage
        TranslationKey string    `gorm:"type:varchar(100);not null;uniqueIndex:idx_group_key" json:"translation_key"` // e.g. beranda, badge, title
        TextID         string    `gorm:"type:text;not null" json:"text_id"`                                          // Bahasa Indonesia
        TextEN         string    `gorm:"type:text;not null" json:"text_en"`                                          // Bahasa Inggris (English)
        Urutan         int       `gorm:"default:0" json:"urutan"`
        CreatedAt      time.Time `json:"created_at"`
        UpdatedAt      time.Time `json:"updated_at"`
}



// Hook GORM User untuk Enkripsi Otomatis AES-256-GCM & Blind Index
func (u *User) BeforeCreate(tx *gorm.DB) error {
        if u.UUID == "" {
                u.UUID = uuid.New().String()
        }
        return nil
}

func (u *User) BeforeSave(tx *gorm.DB) error {
        if u.UUID == "" {
                u.UUID = uuid.New().String()
        }
        cleanNIK := strings.TrimSpace(u.NIK)
        if cleanNIK != "" {
                if !crypto.IsEncryptedString(cleanNIK) {
                        u.NIKIdx = crypto.BlindIndex(cleanNIK)
                        aad := []byte(fmt.Sprintf("users:nik:%s", u.UUID))
                        enc, err := crypto.EncryptString(cleanNIK, aad)
                        if err != nil {
                                return fmt.Errorf("gagal enkripsi NIK: %w", err)
                        }
                        u.NIK = enc
                }
        }
        return nil
}

func (u *User) AfterFind(tx *gorm.DB) error {
        if u.NIK != "" && crypto.IsEncryptedString(u.NIK) {
                aad := []byte(fmt.Sprintf("users:nik:%s", u.UUID))
                dec, err := crypto.DecryptString(u.NIK, aad)
                if err == nil {
                        u.NIK = dec
                }
        }
        return nil
}

// Hook GORM OrangTua untuk Enkripsi Otomatis AES-256-GCM & Blind Index
func (ot *OrangTua) BeforeSave(tx *gorm.DB) error {
        if clean := strings.TrimSpace(ot.NIKAyah); clean != "" {
                ot.NIKAyahIdx = crypto.BlindIndex(clean)
                if !crypto.IsEncryptedString(clean) {
                        aad := []byte(fmt.Sprintf("orang_tuas:nik_ayah:%d", ot.PendaftarID))
                        enc, err := crypto.EncryptString(clean, aad)
                        if err != nil {
                                return fmt.Errorf("gagal enkripsi NIK Ayah: %w", err)
                        }
                        ot.NIKAyah = enc
                }
        }
        if clean := strings.TrimSpace(ot.KKAyah); clean != "" {
                ot.KKAyahIdx = crypto.BlindIndex(clean)
                if !crypto.IsEncryptedString(clean) {
                        aad := []byte(fmt.Sprintf("orang_tuas:kk_ayah:%d", ot.PendaftarID))
                        enc, err := crypto.EncryptString(clean, aad)
                        if err != nil {
                                return fmt.Errorf("gagal enkripsi KK Ayah: %w", err)
                        }
                        ot.KKAyah = enc
                }
        }
        if clean := strings.TrimSpace(ot.NIKIbu); clean != "" {
                ot.NIKIbuIdx = crypto.BlindIndex(clean)
                if !crypto.IsEncryptedString(clean) {
                        aad := []byte(fmt.Sprintf("orang_tuas:nik_ibu:%d", ot.PendaftarID))
                        enc, err := crypto.EncryptString(clean, aad)
                        if err != nil {
                                return fmt.Errorf("gagal enkripsi NIK Ibu: %w", err)
                        }
                        ot.NIKIbu = enc
                }
        }
        if clean := strings.TrimSpace(ot.KKIbu); clean != "" {
                ot.KKIbuIdx = crypto.BlindIndex(clean)
                if !crypto.IsEncryptedString(clean) {
                        aad := []byte(fmt.Sprintf("orang_tuas:kk_ibu:%d", ot.PendaftarID))
                        enc, err := crypto.EncryptString(clean, aad)
                        if err != nil {
                                return fmt.Errorf("gagal enkripsi KK Ibu: %w", err)
                        }
                        ot.KKIbu = enc
                }
        }
        return nil
}

func (ot *OrangTua) AfterFind(tx *gorm.DB) error {
        if ot.NIKAyah != "" && crypto.IsEncryptedString(ot.NIKAyah) {
                aad := []byte(fmt.Sprintf("orang_tuas:nik_ayah:%d", ot.PendaftarID))
                if dec, err := crypto.DecryptString(ot.NIKAyah, aad); err == nil {
                        ot.NIKAyah = dec
                }
        }
        if ot.KKAyah != "" && crypto.IsEncryptedString(ot.KKAyah) {
                aad := []byte(fmt.Sprintf("orang_tuas:kk_ayah:%d", ot.PendaftarID))
                if dec, err := crypto.DecryptString(ot.KKAyah, aad); err == nil {
                        ot.KKAyah = dec
                }
        }
        if ot.NIKIbu != "" && crypto.IsEncryptedString(ot.NIKIbu) {
                aad := []byte(fmt.Sprintf("orang_tuas:nik_ibu:%d", ot.PendaftarID))
                if dec, err := crypto.DecryptString(ot.NIKIbu, aad); err == nil {
                        ot.NIKIbu = dec
                }
        }
        if ot.KKIbu != "" && crypto.IsEncryptedString(ot.KKIbu) {
                aad := []byte(fmt.Sprintf("orang_tuas:kk_ibu:%d", ot.PendaftarID))
                if dec, err := crypto.DecryptString(ot.KKIbu, aad); err == nil {
                        ot.KKIbu = dec
                }
        }
        return nil
}

package response

import (
        "net/http"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

type APIResponse struct {
        Success bool        `json:"success"`
        Message string      `json:"message,omitempty"`
        Data    interface{} `json:"data,omitempty"`
        Error   *ErrorInfo  `json:"error,omitempty"`
}

type ErrorInfo struct {
        Code             string `json:"code"`
        Message          string `json:"message"`
        DeveloperMessage string `json:"developer_message,omitempty"`
}

type PaginationMeta struct {
        Total       int64 `json:"total"`
        Page        int   `json:"page"`
        PerPage     int   `json:"per_page"`
        TotalPages  int   `json:"total_pages"`
}

type PaginatedData struct {
        Items interface{}    `json:"items"`
        Meta  PaginationMeta `json:"meta"`
}

func Success(c *gin.Context, statusCode int, message string, data interface{}) {
        c.JSON(statusCode, APIResponse{
                Success: true,
                Message: message,
                Data:    data,
        })
}

func Error(c *gin.Context, statusCode int, code, userMsg, devMsg string) {
        c.JSON(statusCode, APIResponse{
                Success: false,
                Error: &ErrorInfo{
                        Code:             code,
                        Message:          userMsg,
                        DeveloperMessage: devMsg,
                },
        })
}

func BadRequest(c *gin.Context, userMsg, devMsg string) {
        Error(c, http.StatusBadRequest, "BAD_REQUEST", userMsg, devMsg)
}

func Unauthorized(c *gin.Context, userMsg string) {
        Error(c, http.StatusUnauthorized, "UNAUTHORIZED", userMsg, "Autentikasi diperlukan")
}

func Forbidden(c *gin.Context, userMsg string) {
        Error(c, http.StatusForbidden, "FORBIDDEN", userMsg, "Akses ditolak")
}

func NotFound(c *gin.Context, userMsg string) {
        Error(c, http.StatusNotFound, "NOT_FOUND", userMsg, "Sumber daya tidak ditemukan")
}

func InternalServerError(c *gin.Context, devMsg string) {
        Error(c, http.StatusInternalServerError, "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR", "Terjadi kesalahan internal pada server. Silakan coba lagi nanti.", devMsg)
}

package util

        "crypto/rand"
        "crypto/tls"
        "fmt"
        "io"
        "log"
        "net"
        "net/smtp"
        "pmb-staici-backend/internal/config"
        "strconv"
        "strings"
        "time"
)

// GenerateOTP generates a cryptographically secure 6-digit numeric OTP string
func GenerateOTP() string {
        var table = [...]byte{'1', '2', '3', '4', '5', '6', '7', '8', '9', '0'}
        b := make([]byte, 6)
        n, err := io.ReadAtLeast(rand.Reader, b, 6)
        if n != 6 || err != nil {
                // Fallback timestamp-based RNG
                return fmt.Sprintf("%06d", time.Now().UnixNano()%1000000)
        }
        for i := 0; i < len(b); i++ {
                b[i] = table[int(b[i])%len(table)]
        }
        return string(b)
}
