// sendHTMLEmail is internal helper to deliver email via SMTP
func sendHTMLEmail(cfg *config.Config, toEmail, subject, htmlBody string) error {
        fromName := cfg.SMTPFromName
        if fromName == "" {
                fromName = "PMB STAI Cendekia Insani"
        }
        fromEmail := cfg.SMTPFrom
        if fromEmail == "" {
                fromEmail = cfg.SMTPUser
        }

        headers := make(map[string]string)
        headers["From"] = fmt.Sprintf("%s <%s>", fromName, fromEmail)
        headers["To"] = toEmail
        headers["Subject"] = subject
        headers["MIME-Version"] = "1.0"
        headers["Content-Type"] = "text/html; charset=UTF-8"

        message := ""
        for k, v := range headers {
                message += fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\r\n", k, v)
        }
        message += "\r\n" + htmlBody

        portInt, _ := strconv.Atoi(cfg.SMTPPort)
        if portInt == 0 {
                portInt = 587
        }
        addr := fmt.Sprintf("%s:%d", cfg.SMTPHost, portInt)

        auth := smtp.PlainAuth("", cfg.SMTPUser, cfg.SMTPPass, cfg.SMTPHost)

        // Direct SSL on port 465
        if portInt == 465 {
                tlsConfig := &tls.Config{
                        ServerName: cfg.SMTPHost,
                }
                conn, err := tls.Dial("tcp", addr, tlsConfig)
                if err != nil {
                        return fmt.Errorf("gagal terhubung ke SMTP TLS: %w", err)
                }
                defer conn.Close()

                client, err := smtp.NewClient(conn, cfg.SMTPHost)
                if err != nil {
                        return fmt.Errorf("gagal membuat SMTP client: %w", err)
                }
                defer client.Quit()

                if err = client.Auth(auth); err != nil {
                        return fmt.Errorf("autentikasi SMTP gagal: %w", err)
                }
                if err = client.Mail(fromEmail); err != nil {
                        return fmt.Errorf("SMTP MAIL command gagal: %w", err)
                }
                if err = client.Rcpt(toEmail); err != nil {
                        return fmt.Errorf("SMTP RCPT command gagal: %w", err)
                }
                w, err := client.Data()
                if err != nil {
                        return fmt.Errorf("SMTP DATA command gagal: %w", err)
                }
                _, err = w.Write([]byte(message))
                if err != nil {
                        return fmt.Errorf("gagal menulis email: %w", err)
                }
                err = w.Close()
                if err != nil {
                        return fmt.Errorf("gagal menutup data email: %w", err)
                }
                return nil
        }

        // Standard STARTTLS (Port 587 or 25)
        host, _, _ := net.SplitHostPort(addr)
        client, err := smtp.Dial(addr)
        if err != nil {
                return fmt.Errorf("gagal terhubung ke server SMTP: %w", err)
        }
        defer client.Quit()

        if ok, _ := client.Extension("STARTTLS"); ok {
                config := &tls.Config{ServerName: host}
                if err = client.StartTLS(config); err != nil {
                        return fmt.Errorf("gagal STARTTLS: %w", err)
                }
        }

        if err = client.Auth(auth); err != nil {
                return fmt.Errorf("autentikasi SMTP gagal: %w", err)
        }
        if err = client.Mail(fromEmail); err != nil {
                return fmt.Errorf("SMTP MAIL error: %w", err)
        }
        if err = client.Rcpt(toEmail); err != nil {
                return fmt.Errorf("SMTP RCPT error: %w", err)
        }
        w, err := client.Data()
        if err != nil {
                return fmt.Errorf("SMTP DATA error: %w", err)
        }
        _, err = w.Write([]byte(message))
        if err != nil {
                return fmt.Errorf("gagal menulis data email: %w", err)
        }
        err = w.Close()
        if err != nil {
                return fmt.Errorf("gagal menyelesaikan pengiriman email: %w", err)
        }

        log.Printf("✓ Email berhasil dikirimkan ke %s", toEmail)
        return nil
}

package util

import (
        "errors"
        "regexp"
        "strings"
        "unicode"

        "golang.org/x/crypto/bcrypt"
)

// HashPassword hashes plain text password using bcrypt (cost 10)
func HashPassword(password string) (string, error) {
        bytes, err := bcrypt.GenerateFromPassword([]byte(password), 10)
        return string(bytes), err
}

// CheckPasswordHash compares hash with candidate password
func CheckPasswordHash(password, hash string) bool {
        err := bcrypt.CompareHashAndPassword([]byte(hash), []byte(password))
        return err == nil
}

// Common leaked or easily guessable passwords (lowercase)
var commonLeakedPasswords = map[string]bool{
        "password1234":    true,
        "password12345":   true,
        "password123456":  true,
        "123456789012":    true,
        "1234567890123":   true,
        "qwertyuiopas":    true,
        "qwertyuiopasd":   true,
        "admin12345678":   true,
        "administrator":   true,
        "staici1234567":   true,
        "staicisitubondo": true,
        "situbondo1234":   true,
        "mahasiswabaru":   true,
        "mahasiswastaici": true,
        "rahasia123456":   true,
        "bismillah1234":   true,
        "indonesia1234":   true,
        "welcome123456":   true,
        "iloveyou12345":   true,
        "letmein123456":   true,
        "sunshine12345":   true,
}

// ValidatePasswordStrength checks if password meets security standards:
// - Minimal 12 karakter.
// - Menolak password umum / bocor / karakter berulang.
// - Fleksibel (NIST SP 800-63B): jika >= 16 karakter (passphrase panjang yang kuat), tidak wajib kombinasi kaku.
// - Jika 12 - 15 karakter, wajib memadukan minimal 2 kategori karakter (huruf besar/kecil, angka, simbol).
func ValidatePasswordStrength(password string) error {
        trimmed := strings.TrimSpace(password)

        // 1. Minimal 12 karakter
        if len(trimmed) < 12 {
                return errors.New("kata sandi harus memiliki minimal 12 karakter")
        }

        // 2. Cek password umum / bocor
        lowered := strings.ToLower(trimmed)
        if commonLeakedPasswords[lowered] {
                return errors.New("kata sandi terlalu umum atau berisiko mudah ditebak, silakan gunakan kombinasi lain")
        }

        // 3. Cek karakter tunggal berulang (misal aaaaaaaaaaaa)
        allSame := true
        for i := 1; i < len(trimmed); i++ {
                if trimmed[i] != trimmed[0] {
                        allSame = false
                        break
                }
        }
        if allSame {
                return errors.New("kata sandi tidak boleh hanya terdiri dari satu karakter yang diulang")
        }

        // 4. Hitung keragaman kategori karakter
        var hasLower, hasUpper, hasDigit, hasSpecial bool
        for _, ch := range trimmed {
                switch {
                case unicode.IsLower(ch):
                        hasLower = true
                case unicode.IsUpper(ch):
                        hasUpper = true
                case unicode.IsDigit(ch):
                        hasDigit = true
                default:
                        hasSpecial = true
                }
        }

        categories := 0
        if hasLower {
                categories++
        }
        if hasUpper {
                categories++
        }
        if hasDigit {
                categories++
        }
        if hasSpecial {
                categories++
        }

        // Jika panjang >= 16 karakter (passphrase panjang):
        // Tidak mewajibkan kombinasi simbol secara kaku (NIST SP 800-63B).
        if len(trimmed) >= 16 {
                return nil
        }

        // Jika 12 - 15 karakter: minimal 2 kategori karakter
        if categories < 2 {
                return errors.New("untuk kata sandi di bawah 16 karakter, kombinasikan minimal 2 jenis karakter (huruf besar/kecil, angka, atau simbol)")
        }

        return nil
}

// ValidateNIK checks if NIK has exactly 16 digits
func ValidateNIK(nik string) bool {
        match, _ := regexp.MatchString(`^[0-9]{16}$`, nik)
        return match
}
